322b7(mdo sna tshogs): shi 'phos nas bud med kyi lus spangs te/skyes pa'i dbang po thob nas rtag par rgyun du lha dang /mi rnams kyi nang du skyes te/lan brgyad khri bzhi stong du 'khor los sgyur ba'i rgyal pos byas nas de bzhin gshegs pa rin chen 'od 'phro zhes bya bar
